./linux/flutter/ephemeral/.plugin_symlinks/file_picker/CHANGELOG.md:This prevents error messages for permissions (NSPhotoLibraryUsageDescription, NSAppleMusicUsageDescription, etc.) when publishing to app store connect, in case you don't need either category. This addresses [#783](https://github.com/miguelpruivo/flutter_file_picker/issues/783) in a different way.
./linux/flutter/ephemeral/.plugin_symlinks/file_picker/CHANGELOG.md:Adds the additional parameter `initialDirectory` to configure the initial directory where the dialog should be opened. This parameter is supported for all three dialogs (pick files, pick directory, and save file). The only exception is that the parameter does not work on Windows for the function `getDirectoryPath()`. Please note that this feature has not been implemented for Android and iOS.
./linux/flutter/ephemeral/.plugin_symlinks/file_picker/CHANGELOG.md:Fixes the issue under Windows that the save-file dialog did not open if the specified file name contained an illegal character. Windows prohibits the usage of [reserved characters in file names](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/fileio/naming-a-file#naming-conventions). Now the exception `IllegalCharacterInFileNameException` is thrown if the specified file name contains forbidden characters
./linux/flutter/ephemeral/.plugin_symlinks/file_picker/CHANGELOG.md:Fixes the issue under Windows that the user could select all file types even though a file type filter was enabled. This error existed because the user could select the entry `All Files (*.*)` in the file type filter dropdown. Also, fixes the bug under macOS that users could select files without file extension even when one of the pre-defined file type filters (audio, image, video, or media) was enabled. ([#871](https://github.com/miguelpruivo/flutter_file_picker/issues/871)).

./linux/flutter/ephemeral/.plugin_symlinks/file_picker/CHANGELOG.md:Addresses an issue when comparing files on Web would result in an error due to `path` not being accessible. ([#822](https://github.com/miguelpruivo/flutter_file_picker/issues/822))

This parameter is supported for all three dialogs (pick files, pick directory, and save file). The only exception is that the parameter does not work on Windows for the function `getDirectoryPath()`. Please note that this feature has not been implemented for Android and iOS.
./windows/flutter/ephemeral/.plugin_symlinks/file_picker/CHANGELOG.md:Fixes the issue under Windows that the save-file dialog did not open if the specified file name contained an illegal character. Windows prohibits the usage of [reserved characters in file names](https://docs.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/fileio/naming-a-file#naming-conventions). Now the exception `IllegalCharacterInFileNameException` is thrown if the specified file name contains forbidden characters
./windows/flutter/ephemeral/.plugin_symlinks/file_picker/CHANGELOG.md:Fixes the issue under Windows that the user could select all file types even though a file type filter was enabled. This error existed because the user could select the entry `All Files (*.*)` in the file type filter dropdown. Also, fixes the bug under macOS that users could select files without file extension even when one of the pre-defined file type filters (audio, image, video, or media) was enabled. ([#871](https://github.com/miguelpruivo/flutter_file_picker/issues/871)).
